       ...              S!JMMARY OF UAS SIGHTINGS FOR THE PERIOD 18-2'3 OCT 78

                                                        DURATION                   DESCRIPI'ION

  18 Oct 78      1900         SOUTH BLACKBURN               1hr        Circular light, disc shaped,
                                                                       bright t hen dull cycling -
                                                                       stationary.
